## 2026-11-11 ~22:45 UTC — Maestro Radio DJ fixes (Claude for Swade): stale time of day + garbled ad endings
- Backups: /opt/swadebase/backups/maestro-dj-fixes-20261111-224150 (dj_cue_fix.py, dj_render.py, dj-studio-index.ts, maestro-dj-cue.service, ads/195-202.mp3 originals)
- dj-studio/index.ts writeScript: writer no longer gets local_time (gets day + part_of_day), told never to say a clock time; station_id asks day/part of day only; scripts are cut at a full sentence (fullSentences) instead of mid-word.
- signal/dj_cue_fix.py: station IDs get the exact air time added just before air (ADD_TIME); time check also catches "A M"/"p m" and spelled times ("four forty-three"); OmniRoute timeout 40s -> 60s + 1 retry; re-voiced audio passes clean_tail when >150s lead.
- signal/clean_tail.py (new): transcribes rendered audio, and if it does not end on the script's last words, steps back through pauses to the clean cut + 0.18s fade. Fails open (returns audio unchanged).
- signal/dj_render.py: new breaks pass clean_tail before upload.
- maestro-dj-cue.service TimeoutStartSec 170 -> 420.
- Engine station 1 commercials replaced in place: 198 sssbrain.mp3 33.0s -> 26.0s (babble removed after "...your own SSS Brain."), 196 videosaywhat.mp3 28.2s -> 25.1s (garbled URL removed after "Find out what they really said."). Others (195,199,200,201,202) transcribed clean, untouched.
- Rollback: copy files back from /opt/swadebase/backups/maestro-dj-fixes-20261111-224150; re-upload ads via POST /api/station/1/files same path.
